Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cosmology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 400 and the invariant I(A,B) = 486 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to cosmology theory, we decompose the problem into 400 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 486. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
